# Original-file locator

Every original occurrence below is preserved unchanged. Equal-byte duplicates remain separate files. Paths in the left column describe the previous checkout; links in the right column open their preserved destinations.

Use this locator when a historical document mentions a former relative path. Scientific originals have not been rewritten to change their links.
